31011:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

30993:         }

30999:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
31000:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
31001:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
31002:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
31003:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}

31005:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
31006:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
31007:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
31008:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

31011:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

31047:   (function installModalResilience(){
31048:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31049:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31050:       if(!ac)return;
31051:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31052:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31053:       e.preventDefault();
31054:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31055:     },true);
31056:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31057:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31058:       if(!root)return;
31059:       e.preventDefault();
31060:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31061:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31062:     });
31063:     try{
31064:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31065:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31066:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31067:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31068:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31069:       });
31070:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31071:     }catch(e){}
31072:   }());

28682: function _exportReportCsv() {
28683:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28684: 
28685:     if (!resultDiv) {
28686:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
28687:         return;
28688:     }
28689: 
28690:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
28691: 
28692:     if (!table) {
28693:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
28694:         return;
28695:     }
28696: 
28697:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
28698:     var csv = [];
28699: 
28700:     function csvCell(value) {
28701:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
28702:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
28703:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
28704: 
28705:         return '"' + s + '"';
28706:     }
28707: 
28708:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
28709:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
28710:         var line = [];
28711: 
28712:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
28713:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
28714:         }
28715: 
28716:         csv.push(line.join(','));
28717:     }
28718: 
28719:     var blob = new Blob(
28720:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
28721:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
28722:     );
28723: 
28724:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
28725:     var a = document.createElement('a');
28726: 
28727:     a.href = url;
28728:     a.download =
28729:         'rawaea-report-' +
28730:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
28731:         '-' +
28732:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
28733:         '.csv';
28734: 
28735:     document.body.appendChild(a);
28736:     a.click();
28737:     document.body.removeChild(a);
28738: 
28739:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
28740: }
28741: function _printReport() {
28742:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28743: 
28744:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
28745:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
28746:         return;
28747:     }
28748: 
28749:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
28750: 
28751:     if (!printWindow) {
28752:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
28753:         return;
28754:     }
28755: 
28756:     var html =
28757:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
28758:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
28759:         '<head>' +
28760:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
28761:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
28762:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
28763:         '<style>' +
28764:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
28765:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
28766:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
28767:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
28768:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
28769:         '</style>' +
28770:         '</head>' +
28771:         '<body>' +
28772:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
28773:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
28774:         '</body></html>';
28775: 
28776:     printWindow.document.open();
28777:     printWindow.document.write(html);
28778:     printWindow.document.close();
28779: }

30947:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}

31010:   async function employeesTab(cn){await loadPeople();var rows=H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'});cn.innerHTML=card('دليل الموظفين','Employee 360 من مركز واحد','<div class="flex gap-2 mb-5"><input id="hr-emp-search" class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border" placeholder="بحث بالاسم أو البريد أو الرقم أو الوظيفة">'+btn('ملف موظف','new-profile')+'</div><div id="hr-emp-grid" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">'+rows.map(function(e){var total=num(e.basic_salary)+num(e.housing_allowance)+num(e.transport_allowance)+num(e.other_allowance)-num(e.default_deduction);return '<article data-eid="'+esc(e.id)+'" class="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black">'+esc((e.name||'?')[0])+'</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-black truncate">'+esc(e.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</div></div></div><div class="mt-4">'+employeeMeta(e)+'</div><div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t flex justify-between text-sm"><span class="text-slate-500">التعويض الحالي</span><b class="text-indigo-700">'+money(total)+' EGP</b></div></article>'}).join('')+'</div>');var s=E('hr-emp-search');if(s)s.oninput=function(){var v=s.value.toLowerCase();cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){var e=rows.filter(function(x){return x.id===el.getAttribute('data-eid')})[0]||{};var h=[e.name,e.email,e.employee_number,e.job_title,e.department_name,e.position_name].join(' ').toLowerCase();el.style.display=!v||h.indexOf(v)>-1?'':'none'})};cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){el.onclick=function(){open360(el.getAttribute('data-eid'))}})}

31046:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31047:   (function installModalResilience(){
31048:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31049:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31050:       if(!ac)return;
31051:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31052:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31053:       e.preventDefault();
31054:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31055:     },true);
31056:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31057:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31058:       if(!root)return;
31059:       e.preventDefault();
31060:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31061:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31062:     });
31063:     try{
31064:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31065:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31066:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31067:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31068:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31069:       });
31070:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31071:     }catch(e){}
31072:   }());

31080: var RW_CRM = (function() {
31081:     'use strict';
31082: 
31083:     var state = {
31084:         customers: [],
31085:         assignees: [],
31086:         kpi: {},
31087:         search: '',
31088:         activeOnly: false,
31089:         searchTimer: null
31090:     };
31091: 
31092:     function _esc(s) {
31093:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
31094:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
31095:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
31096:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
31097:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
31098:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
31099:     }
31100: 
31101:     function _fmtNum(n) {
31102:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
31103:     }
31104: 
31105:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
31106:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
31107:     }
31108: 
31109:     function _today() {
31110:         var d = new Date();
31111:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
31112:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
31113:     }
31114: 
31115:     function _statusLabel(s) {
31116:         var map = {
31117:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
31118:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
31119:             completed: 'مكتملة',
31120:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
31121:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
31122:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
31123:         };
31124:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
31125:     }
31126: 
31127:     function _statusClass(s) {
31128:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
31129:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
31130:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
31131:     }
31132: 
31133:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
31134:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
31135:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
31136:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
31137:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
31138:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
31139:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
31140:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
31141:     }
31142: 
31143:     async function _loadDirectory() {
31144:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
31145:             p_search: state.search || null,
31146:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
31147:             p_limit: 200,
31148:             p_offset: 0
31149:         });
31150:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
31151: 
31152:         var payload = res.data || {};
31153:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
31154:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
31155:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
31156:         return payload;
31157:     }
31158: 
31159:     function _renderKpis() {
31160:         var k = state.kpi || {};
31161:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
31162:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31163:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31164:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
31165:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31166:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31167:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
31168:         '</div>';
31169:     }
31170: 
31171:     function _renderTable() {
31172:         if (!state.customers.length) {
31173:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
31174:         }
31175: 
31176:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
31177:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
31178:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
31179:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
31180:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
31181:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
31182:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
31183:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
31184:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
31185:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
31186:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
31187: 
31188:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
31189:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
31190:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
31191:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
31192: 
31193:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
31194:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
31195:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31196:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31197:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
31198:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
31199:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
31200:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
31201:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
31202:             '</tr>';
31203:         }
31204: 
31205:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
31206:     }
31207: 
31208:     function _bindDirectory() {
31209:         var search = byId('crm-search');
31210:         if (search) {
31211:             search.value = state.search;
31212:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
31213:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
31214:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
31215:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
31216:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
31217:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
31218:                         _bindOpen360();
31219:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
31220:                     }).catch(function(e) {
31221:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
31222:                     });
31223:                 }, 250);
31224:             });
31225:         }

31046:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31047:   (function installModalResilience(){
31048:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31049:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31050:       if(!ac)return;
31051:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31052:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31053:       e.preventDefault();
31054:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31055:     },true);
31056:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31057:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31058:       if(!root)return;
31059:       e.preventDefault();
31060:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31061:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31062:     });
31063:     try{
31064:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31065:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31066:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31067:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31068:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31069:       });
31070:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31071:     }catch(e){}
31072:   }());
